                "description": "\u003ch1\u003e\u003cu\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDENVER - Vehicles on Behalf of Colorado Auto Recovery\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/h1\u003e\r\n\r\n\u003cp\u003eINSPECTION: Wednesday, May 13th 2:00 - 4:00 PM MT\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nSTAGGERED BIDDING ENDS: Wednesday, May 13th 5:00 PM MT\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPAYMENT DEADLINE: Thursday, May 14th no later than 12:00 PM MT\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nREMOVAL: Thursday, May 14th 2:00 - 4:00 PM MT. \u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nADDRESS: 281 East 55th Avenue, Denver, CO 80216\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n10% buyer's premium on all purchases.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPURCHASER OR AUTHORIZED THIRD PARTY TO RELOCATE-CHECK IN WITH AUCTION TEAM UPON ARRIVAL with a paid invoice.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eMATTERS TO CONSIDER BEFORE PARTICIPATING\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cu\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e10% buyer's premium is charged on all purchases.\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/u\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nSome of these vehicles may be pulled from auction.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPlease fully review all photographs and run any/all Carfax, Experian, AutoCheck or other reports prior to bidding to feel comfortable with your purchase(s).\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nThe vehicles being auctioned may be subject to open recall notices. It is the buyer’s sole responsibility to check for and act upon any applicable recall notices. Recalls and other vehicle safety information is available at the national highway traffic safety administration website: \u003ca href=\"http://www.nhtsa.gov/recalls\" rel=\"nofollow\" class=\"iab\"\u003ewww.nhtsa.gov/recalls\u003c/a\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eREGISTRATION INSTRUCTIONS\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nDickensheet \u0026amp; Associates, Inc. reserve the right to accept or reject any registration. Only complete and accurate registration will be approved. PO Box’s will automatically be rejected.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003ePAYMENT INSTRUCTIONS\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAn invoice will be forwarded to the registration email address following the conclusion of the auction.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eACCEPTED FORMS OF PAYMENT:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eCASH\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003e LOCATIONS FOR PAYMENT\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eAuctioneer Location\u003c/strong\u003e: 1501 W. Wesley Ave., Denver, CO 80223\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eVectra Bank\u003c/strong\u003e: Accepted at all Vectra Bank locations throughout the State of Colorado. Be aware these locations may be closed on Holiday’s.\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPlease utilize the information below for the Vectra Bank location nearest to you.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003ca href=\"https://www.vectrabank.com/branch-locator/\" rel=\"nofollow\" class=\"iab\"\u003eVectra Bank Colorado Branch and ATM Locations\u003c/a\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eZELLE-\u003c/strong\u003ePlease send transfer info to \u003cem\u003e\u003cu\u003e\u003ca href=\"mailto:sales@dickensheet.com\" rel=\"nofollow\" class=\"iab\"\u003esales@dickensheet.com\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/em\u003e, with your invoice number noted in the memo section. All Zelle payments would need to be completed by successful bidders to assure the payment is noted in Auctioneer’s account prior to payment deadline. \u003cstrong\u003e\u003cem\u003e\u003cu\u003eBe aware \u003c/u\u003eZelle\u003cu\u003e transfers can take 24 hours to process and is the buyer’s responsibility to ensure payment is received on time.\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e Please note that while Zelle itself does not charge a fee for its services, some banks or financial institutions may apply transaction fees or other charges on your end. Please review the terms and conditions of your bank or contact them directly to confirm whether additional fees may apply when using Zelle.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eCARD PAYMENTS\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\nVisa, MasterCard, Discover credit cards will be accepted for a maximum of $500.00 to the card utilized for registration, 2% convenience fee applies.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eWIRE TRANSFERS\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAll wire transfers must be received by the auctioneers no later than the stated time outlined above. Be aware wire transfers can take 24 hours to process and is buyer’s responsibility to ensure payment is received on time.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003ePAPERWORK FOR VEHICLE\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAll paperwork shall be filled out in registered bidders name and address unless a change is requested. It is the buyer’s responsibility to ensure all paperwork is accurate.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAn AI assisted software was used to write portions of the Details of the Auction Info.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPLEASE NOTE: PURCHASER HAS 30 DAYS FROM RECEIPT OF TITLE PAPERWORK TO REGISTER VEHICLE WITH THE APPROPRIATE MOTOR VEHICLE DEPARTMENT, AFTER THAT TIME PERIOD, THERE IS NO GUARANTEE THAT ANY CORRECTIONS/CHANGES CAN BE MADE, OR THE SELLER/AUCTIONEER WILL BE ABLE TO ASSIST WITH ANY ISSUES REGARDING TRANSFER OF TITLE.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nDickensheet \u0026amp; Associates, Inc.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n1501 West Wesley Avenue\u003cbr\u003e\r\nDenver, CO 80223\u003cbr\u003e\r\nTelephone: (303) 934-8322\u003cbr\u003e\r\nToll Free: (877) 284-0338\u003cbr\u003e\r\nFax: (303) 934-8252\u003cbr\u003e\r\nE-mail: \u003ca href=\"mailto:customerservice@dickensheet.com\" rel=\"nofollow\" class=\"iab\"\u003ecustomerservice@dickensheet.com\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/p\u003e",

                "description": "\u003ch1\u003e\u003cu\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDENVER-Vehicles, Travel Trailers, Boat On Behalf Of The US Bankruptcy Court, Others\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/h1\u003e\r\n\r\n\u003cp\u003eON BEHALF OF THE UNITED STATES BANKRUPTCY COURT\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nINSPECTION: Wednesday, May 20th 2:00 PM - 4:00 PM MT\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nSTAGGERED ONLINE BIDDING ENDS: Wednesday, May 20th 5:00 PM MT\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPAYMENT DEADLINE: Thursday, May 21st no later than 5:00 PM MT\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nREMOVAL: Monday-Friday 9:00 AM - 4:00 PM MT by Thursday, May 28th, 2026\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nADDRESS: 1501 W Wesley Avenue, Denver, CO 80223\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n10% buyer's premium on all lots unless otherwise noted.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nSome of these vehicles may be pulled from auction.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPlease fully review all photographs and run any/all Carfax, Experian, AutoCheck or other reports prior to bidding to feel comfortable with your purchase(s).\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eCATALYTIC CONVERTER DISCLOSURE: \u003c/strong\u003eDue to the nature of the origin of these vehicles, the Colorado Department of Revenue, nor Dickensheet \u0026amp; Associates, Inc. make any guarantee that any of these vehicles still have their catalytic converter. There will be no refunds or any kind of discount given if the vehicle is missing its catalytic converter.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nThe vehicles being auctioned may be subject to open recall notices. It is the buyer’s sole responsibility to check for and act upon any applicable recall notices. Recalls and other vehicle safety information is available at the national highway traffic safety administration website: \u003ca href=\"http://www.nhtsa.gov/recalls\" rel=\"nofollow\" class=\"iab\"\u003ewww.nhtsa.gov/recalls\u003c/a\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nUnless stated in the description of the posting, keys are not available with vehicle.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPURCHASER will be responsible for emissions, where applicable. Neither the Colorado Department of Revenue nor Dickensheet \u0026amp; Associates, Inc. guarantee emissions or that all equipment is located on the vehicle to obtain emissions.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eREGISTRATION INSTRUCTIONS\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nDickensheet \u0026amp; Associates, Inc. reserve the right to accept or reject any registration. Only complete and accurate registration will be approved. PO Box’s will automatically be rejected.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003ePAYMENT INSTRUCTIONS\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAn invoice will be forwarded to the registration email address following the conclusion of the auction. It is the bidders responsibility to assure invoice is received.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eACCEPTED FORMS OF PAYMENT:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eCASH\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003e LOCATIONS FOR PAYMENT\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eAuctioneer Location\u003c/strong\u003e: 1501 W. Wesley Ave., Denver, CO 80223\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eVectra Bank\u003c/strong\u003e: Accepted at all Vectra Bank locations throughout the State of Colorado. Be aware these locations may be closed on Holiday’s.\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPlease utilize the information below for the Vectra Bank location nearest to you.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003ca href=\"https://www.vectrabank.com/branch-locator/\" rel=\"nofollow\" class=\"iab\"\u003eVectra Bank Colorado Branch and ATM Locations\u003c/a\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eZELLE-\u003c/strong\u003ePlease send transfer info to \u003cem\u003e\u003cu\u003e\u003ca href=\"mailto:sales@dickensheet.com\" rel=\"nofollow\" class=\"iab\"\u003esales@dickensheet.com\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/em\u003e, with your invoice number noted in the memo section. All Zelle payments would need to be completed by successful bidders to assure the payment is noted in Auctioneer’s account prior to payment deadline. \u003cstrong\u003e\u003cem\u003e\u003cu\u003eBe aware \u003c/u\u003eZelle\u003cu\u003e transfers can take 24 hours to process and is the buyer’s responsibility to ensure payment is received on time.\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e Please note that while Zelle itself does not charge a fee for its services, some banks or financial institutions may apply transaction fees or other charges on your end. Please review the terms and conditions of your bank or contact them directly to confirm whether additional fees may apply when using Zelle.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eCARD PAYMENTS\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\nCredit cards will not be accepted at this auction.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eWIRE TRANSFERS\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAll wire transfers must be received by the auctioneers no later than the stated time outlined above. Be aware wire transfers can take 24 hours to process and is buyer’s responsibility to ensure payment is received on time.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003ePAPERWORK FOR VEHICLE\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAll paperwork shall be filled out in registered bidders name and address unless a change is requested. It is the buyer’s responsibility to ensure all paperwork is accurate.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAn AI assisted software was used to write portions of the Details of the Auction Info.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPLEASE NOTE: PURCHASER HAS 30 DAYS FROM RECEIPT OF CERTIFICATE OF PURCHASE PAPERWORK TO REGISTER VEHICLE WITH THE APPROPRIATE MOTOR VEHICLE DEPARTMENT, AFTER THAT TIME PERIOD, THERE IS NO GUARANTEE THAT ANY CORRECTIONS/CHANGES CAN BE MADE, OR THE SELLER/AUCTIONEER WILL BE ABLE TO ASSIST WITH ANY ISSUES.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n10% buyer's premium on all lots unless otherwise noted.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nDickensheet \u0026amp; Associates, Inc.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n1501 West Wesley Avenue\u003cbr\u003e\r\nDenver, CO 80223\u003cbr\u003e\r\nTelephone: (303) 934-8322\u003cbr\u003e\r\nToll Free: (877) 284-0338\u003cbr\u003e\r\nFax: (303) 934-8252\u003cbr\u003e\r\nE-mail: \u003ca href=\"mailto:customerservice@dickensheet.com\" rel=\"nofollow\" class=\"iab\"\u003ecustomerservice@dickensheet.com\u003c/a\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n \u003c/p\u003e\r\n\u003cp\u003e \u003c/p\u003e",

PO Box’s will automatically be rejected.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003ePAYMENT INSTRUCTIONS\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAn invoice will be forwarded to the registration email address following the conclusion of the auction.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eACCEPTED FORMS OF PAYMENT:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eCASH\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003e LOCATIONS FOR PAYMENT\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eAuctioneer Location\u003c/strong\u003e: 1501 W. Wesley Ave., Denver, CO 80223\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eVectra Bank\u003c/strong\u003e: Accepted at all Vectra Bank locations throughout the State of Colorado. Be aware these locations may be closed on Holiday’s.\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPlease utilize the information below for the Vectra Bank location nearest to you.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003ca href=\"https://www.vectrabank.com/branch-locator/\" rel=\"nofollow\" class=\"iab\"\u003eVectra Bank Colorado Branch and ATM Locations\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\r\n\u003cp\u003e\u003cu\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eZELLE\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/u\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPlease send transfer info to \u003cem\u003e\u003ca href=\"mailto:sales@dickensheet.com\" rel=\"nofollow\" class=\"iab\"\u003esales@dickensheet.com\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/em\u003e, with your invoice number noted in the memo section. All Zelle payments would need to be completed by successful bidders to assure the payment is noted in Auctioneer’s account prior to payment deadline. \u003cstrong\u003e\u003cem\u003e\u003cu\u003eBe aware \u003c/u\u003eZelle\u003cu\u003e transfers can take 24 hours to process and is the buyer’s responsibility to ensure payment is received on time.\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e Please note that while Zelle itself does not charge a fee for its services, some banks or financial institutions may apply transaction fees or other charges on your end. To use a credit card, buyer must contact auctioneer to authorize charge.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eWIRE TRANSFERS\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAll wire transfers must be received by the auctioneers no later than the stated time outlined above.                 "description": "\u003ch1\u003e\u003cu\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTHORNTON - Meati Foods - Newer Equipment and More From Over 100,000 Sq Ft Facility\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/h1\u003e\r\n\r\n\u003cp\u003eON BEHALF OF ADAMS COUNTY-All items will be offered in bulk, then piecemeal and awarded in the highest manner.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nMeati Foods is a Colorado‑born food‑tech company that created whole‑food, mycelium‑based “meat” products—cutlets, steaks, and breakfast patties—made almost entirely from mushroom root (mycelium).                 "status": "complete",
                "scheduled_end_time": "2026-04-27T20:00:00.000Z",

                "tag_line": "Aquila Services Corporation dba Denver Metro Protective Services",
                "location": null,
                "description": "\u003ch1\u003e\u003cu\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAquila Services Corporation dba Denver Metro Protective Services\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/h1\u003e\r\n\r\n\u003cp\u003eOn Behalf of The Colorado Department of Revenue-ALL ITEMS WILL BE OFFERED IN BULK, THEN PIECEMEAL AND AWARDED IN THE HIGHEST MANNER.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nINSPECTION: Monday, April 27th 10:00 AM – 12:00 PM MT\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nSTAGGERED ONLINE BIDDING ENDS: Monday, April 27th 2:00 PM MT\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPAYMENT DEADLINE: Tuesday, April 28th no later than 5:00 PM MT\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nREMOVAL: Tuesday, April 28th through Thursday, April 30th 9:00 AM – 5:00 PM MT\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nADDRESS: 1501 W Wesley Avenue, Denver, CO 80223\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPURCHASER OR AUTHORIZED THIRD PARTY TO RELOCATE-CHECK IN WITH AUCTION TEAM UPON ARRIVAL with a paid invoice.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eMATTERS TO CONSIDER BEFORE PARTICIPATING\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cu\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e10% buyer's premium is charged on all purchases.\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/u\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAPPLICABLE SALES TAX WILL BE COLLECTED AT PURCHASER'S LOCAL MOTOR VEHICLE DEPARTMENT ON APPLICABLE ITEMS. DEALERS-PLEASE SEND OVER A COPY OF YOUR DEALER'S LICENSE TO ASSURE PAPERWORK IS COMPLETED IN NAME CORRECTLY. Thank you.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nSome of these vehicles may be pulled from auction.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPlease fully review all photographs and run any/all Carfax, Experian, AutoCheck or other reports prior to bidding to feel comfortable with your purchase(s).\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eCATALYTIC CONVERTER DISCLOSURE: \u003c/strong\u003eDue to the nature of the origin of these vehicles, the Colorado Department of Revenue, nor Dickensheet \u0026amp; Associates, Inc. make any guarantee that any of these vehicles still have their catalytic converter. There will be no refunds or any kind of discount given if the vehicle is missing its catalytic converter.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nThe vehicles being auctioned may be subject to open recall notices. It is the buyer’s sole responsibility to check for and act upon any applicable recall notices. Recalls and other vehicle safety information is available at the national highway traffic safety administration website: \u003ca href=\"http://www.nhtsa.gov/recalls\" rel=\"nofollow\" class=\"iab\"\u003ewww.nhtsa.gov/recalls\u003c/a\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nUnless stated in the description of the posting, keys are not available with vehicle.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPURCHASER will be responsible for emissions, where applicable. Neither the Colorado Department of Revenue nor Dickensheet \u0026amp; Associates, Inc. guarantee emissions or that all equipment is located on the vehicle to obtain emissions.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eREGISTRATION INSTRUCTIONS\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nDickensheet \u0026amp; Associates, Inc. reserve the right to accept or reject any registration. Only complete and accurate registration will be approved. PO Box’s will automatically be rejected.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003ePAYMENT INSTRUCTIONS\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAn invoice will be forwarded to the registration email address following the conclusion of the auction. It is the bidders responsibility to assure invoice is received.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eACCEPTED FORMS OF PAYMENT:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eCASH\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003e LOCATIONS FOR PAYMENT\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eAuctioneer Location\u003c/strong\u003e: 1501 W. Wesley Ave., Denver, CO 80223\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eVectra Bank\u003c/strong\u003e: Accepted at all Vectra Bank locations throughout the State of Colorado. Be aware these locations may be closed on Holiday’s.\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPlease utilize the information below for the Vectra Bank location nearest to you.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003ca href=\"https://www.vectrabank.com/branch-locator/\" rel=\"nofollow\" class=\"iab\"\u003eVectra Bank Colorado Branch and ATM Locations\u003c/a\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eZELLE-\u003c/strong\u003ePlease send transfer info to \u003cem\u003e\u003cu\u003e\u003ca href=\"mailto:sales@dickensheet.com\" rel=\"nofollow\" class=\"iab\"\u003esales@dickensheet.com\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/em\u003e, with your invoice number noted in the memo section. All Zelle payments would need to be completed by successful bidders to assure the payment is noted in Auctioneer’s account prior to payment deadline. \u003cstrong\u003e\u003cem\u003e\u003cu\u003eBe aware \u003c/u\u003eZelle\u003cu\u003e transfers can take 24 hours to process and is the buyer’s responsibility to ensure payment is received on time.\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e Please note that while Zelle itself does not charge a fee for its services, some banks or financial institutions may apply transaction fees or other charges on your end. Please review the terms and conditions of your bank or contact them directly to confirm whether additional fees may apply when using Zelle.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eCARD PAYMENTS\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\nCredit cards will not be accepted at this auction.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eWIRE TRANSFERS\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAll wire transfers must be received by the auctioneers no later than the stated time outlined above. Be aware wire transfers can take 24 hours to process and is buyer’s responsibility to ensure payment is received on time.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003ePAPERWORK FOR VEHICLE\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAll paperwork shall be filled out in registered bidders name and address unless a change is requested. It is the buyer’s responsibility to ensure all paperwork is accurate.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAn AI assisted software was used to write portions of the Details of the Auction Info.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPLEASE NOTE: PURCHASER HAS 30 DAYS FROM RECEIPT OF CERTIFICATE OF PURCHASE PAPERWORK TO REGISTER VEHICLE WITH THE APPROPRIATE MOTOR VEHICLE DEPARTMENT, AFTER THAT TIME PERIOD, THERE IS NO GUARANTEE THAT ANY CORRECTIONS/CHANGES CAN BE MADE, OR THE SELLER/AUCTIONEER WILL BE ABLE TO ASSIST WITH ANY ISSUES.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nDickensheet \u0026amp; Associates, Inc.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n1501 West Wesley Avenue\u003cbr\u003e\r\nDenver, CO 80223\u003cbr\u003e\r\nTelephone: (303) 934-8322\u003cbr\u003e\r\nToll Free: (877) 284-0338\u003cbr\u003e\r\nFax: (303) 934-8252\u003cbr\u003e\r\nE-mail: \u003ca href=\"mailto:customerservice@dickensheet.com\" rel=\"nofollow\" class=\"iab\"\u003ecustomerservice@dickensheet.com\u003c/a\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n \u003c/p\u003e\r\n\u003cp\u003e \u003c/p\u003e",

                "description": "\u003ch1\u003e\u003cu\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDENVER - Coins, Handbags, Cameras and More\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/h1\u003e\r\n\r\n\u003cp\u003eINSPECTION: Wednesday, April 22nd 10:00 AM - 12:00 PM MT\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nSTAGGERED ONLINE BIDDING ENDS: Wednesday, April 22nd 2:00 PM MT\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nREMOVAL: Thursday, April 23rd and Friday, April 24th 9:00 AM - 5:00 PM MT\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nADDRESS: 1501 W Wesley Avenue, Denver, CO 80223\u003c/p\u003e\r\n\u003cp\u003eAny and all issues need to be fully resolved prior to leaving auction site.\u003c/p\u003e\r\n\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eMATTERS TO CONSIDER BEFORE PARTICIPATING\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cu\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e13% buyer's premium is charged on all purchases for cash, wire transfer, Zelle, cashier's check.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n15% buyer's premium for credit card and convenience fee-Visa, MasterCard, Discover, American Express.\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/u\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eREGISTRATION INSTRUCTIONS\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nDickensheet \u0026amp; Associates, Inc. reserve the right to accept or reject any registration. Only complete and accurate registration will be approved. Be aware these locations may be closed on Holiday’s.\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPlease utilize the information below for the Vectra Bank location nearest to you.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003ca href=\"https://www.vectrabank.com/branch-locator/\" rel=\"nofollow\" class=\"iab\"\u003eVectra Bank Colorado Branch and ATM Locations\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\r\n\u003cp\u003e\u003cu\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eZELLE\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/u\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPlease send transfer info to \u003cem\u003e\u003ca href=\"mailto:sales@dickensheet.com\" rel=\"nofollow\" class=\"iab\"\u003esales@dickensheet.com\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/em\u003e, with your invoice number noted in the memo section. All Zelle payments would need to be completed by successful bidders to assure the payment is noted in Auctioneer’s account prior to payment deadline. \u003cstrong\u003e\u003cem\u003e\u003cu\u003eBe aware \u003c/u\u003eZelle\u003cu\u003e transfers can take 24 hours to process and is the buyer’s responsibility to ensure payment is received on time.\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e Please note that while Zelle itself does not charge a fee for its services, some banks or financial institutions may apply transaction fees or other charges on your end. No exceptions.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPURCHASER OR AUTHORIZED THIRD PARTY TO RELOCATE-CHECK IN WITH AUCTION TEAM UPON ARRIVAL with a paid invoice.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eMATTERS TO CONSIDER BEFORE PARTICIPATING\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cu\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e10% buyer's premium is charged on all purchases unless otherwise noted in the lot description.\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/u\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nTITLED ITEMS-APPLICABLE SALES TAX WILL BE COLLECTED AT PURCHASER'S LOCAL MOTOR VEHICLE DEPARTMENT. Thank you.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nSome of these vehicles may be pulled from auction.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPlease fully review all photographs and run any/all Carfax, Experian, AutoCheck or other reports prior to bidding to feel comfortable with your purchase(s).\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nThe vehicles being auctioned may be subject to open recall notices.                 "description": "\u003ch1\u003e\u003cu\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDENVER - April Wesley Vehicle Auction\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/h1\u003e\r\n\r\n\u003cp\u003eON BEHALF OF ESTATES, LOCAL TOWING COMPANIES AND OTHERS\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nINSPECTION: Thursday, April 16th 10:00 AM - 12:00 PM MT\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nSTAGGERED ONLINE BIDDING ENDS: Thursday, April 16th 2:00 PM MT\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPAYMENT DEADLINE:Friday, April 17th no later than 5:00 PM MT\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nREMOVAL: No later than Thursday, April 23rd by 5:00 PM MT\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nADDRESS: 1501 W Wesley Avenue, Denver, CO 80223\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n10% buyer's premum on all lots unless otherwise noted.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPURCHASER OR AUTHORIZED THIRD PARTY TO RELOCATE-CHECK IN WITH AUCTION TEAM UPON ARRIVAL with a paid invoice.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eMATTERS TO CONSIDER BEFORE PARTICIPATING\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cu\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e10% buyer's premium is charged on all purchases unless otherwise noted in the lot description.\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/u\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAPPLICABLE SALES TAX WILL BE COLLECTED AT PURCHASER'S LOCAL MOTOR VEHICLE DEPARTMENT. DEALERS-PLEASE SEND OVER A COPY OF YOUR DEALER'S LICENSE TO ASSURE PAPERWORK IS COMPLETED IN NAME CORRECTLY. Thank you.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nSome of these vehicles may be pulled from auction.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPlease fully review all photographs and run any/all Carfax, Experian, AutoCheck or other reports prior to bidding to feel comfortable with your purchase(s).\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nThe vehicles being auctioned may be subject to open recall notices. It is the buyer’s sole responsibility to check for and act upon any applicable recall notices. Recalls and other vehicle safety information is available at the national highway traffic safety administration website: \u003ca href=\"http://www.nhtsa.gov/recalls\" rel=\"nofollow\" class=\"iab\"\u003ewww.nhtsa.gov/recalls\u003c/a\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eREGISTRATION INSTRUCTIONS\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nDickensheet \u0026amp; Associates, Inc. reserve the right to accept or reject any registration. Only complete and accurate registration will be approved. PO Box’s will automatically be rejected.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003ePAYMENT INSTRUCTIONS\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAn invoice will be forwarded to the registration email address following the conclusion of the auction.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eACCEPTED FORMS OF PAYMENT:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eCASH\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003e LOCATIONS FOR PAYMENT\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eAuctioneer Location\u003c/strong\u003e: 1501 W. Wesley Ave., Denver, CO 80223\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eVectra Bank\u003c/strong\u003e: Accepted at all Vectra Bank locations throughout the State of Colorado. Be aware these locations may be closed on Holiday’s.\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPlease utilize the information below for the Vectra Bank location nearest to you.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003ca href=\"https://www.vectrabank.com/branch-locator/\" rel=\"nofollow\" class=\"iab\"\u003eVectra Bank Colorado Branch and ATM Locations\u003c/a\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003eZelle-\u003c/strong\u003ePlease send transfer info to \u003cem\u003e\u003cu\u003e\u003ca href=\"mailto:sales@dickensheet.com\" rel=\"nofollow\" class=\"iab\"\u003esales@dickensheet.com\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/em\u003e, with your invoice number noted in the memo section. All Zelle payments would need to be completed by successful bidders to assure the payment is noted in Auctioneer’s account prior to payment deadline. \u003cstrong\u003e\u003cem\u003e\u003cu\u003eBe aware \u003c/u\u003eZelle\u003cu\u003e transfers can take 24 hours to process and is the buyer’s responsibility to ensure payment is received on time.\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e Please note that while Zelle itself does not charge a fee for its services, some banks or financial institutions may apply transaction fees or other charges on your end. Please review the terms and conditions of your bank or contact them directly to confirm whether additional fees may apply when using Zelle.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eCARD PAYMENTS\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nCredit cards will not be accepted at this auction.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eWIRE TRANSFERS\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAll wire transfers must be received by the auctioneers no later than the stated time outlined above. Be aware wire transfers can take 24 hours to process and is buyer’s responsibility to ensure payment is received on time.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003ePAPERWORK FOR VEHICLE\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAll paperwork shall be filled out in registered bidders name and address unless a change is requested. It is the buyer’s responsibility to ensure all paperwork is accurate.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nAn AI assisted software was used to write portions of the Details of the Auction Info.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nPLEASE NOTE: PURCHASER HAS 30 DAYS FROM RECEIPT OF TITLE PAPERWORK TO REGISTER VEHICLE WITH THE APPROPRIATE MOTOR VEHICLE DEPARTMENT, AFTER THAT TIME PERIOD, THERE IS NO GUARANTEE THAT ANY CORRECTIONS/CHANGES CAN BE MADE, OR THE SELLER/AUCTIONEER WILL BE ABLE TO ASSIST WITH ANY ISSUES REGARDING TRANSFER OF TITLE.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n\u003cbr\u003e\r\nDickensheet \u0026amp; Associates, Inc.\u003cbr\u003e\r\n1501 West Wesley Avenue\u003cbr\u003e\r\nDenver, CO 80223\u003cbr\u003e\r\nTelephone: (303) 934-8322\u003cbr\u003e\r\nToll Free: (877) 284-0338\u003cbr\u003e\r\nFax: (303) 934-8252\u003c/p\u003e\r\n\u003cp\u003e \u003c/p\u003e",
